Conclusion: 本文提出的框架首次成功解决了人脸重演中的跨身份舌部动态传输问题，通过专用的分割与合成流程，有效弥补了现有系统在口腔内部细节生成上的不足，并在定量指标和感知质量上取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Modern face reenactment systems achieve impressive pose and expression transfer using geometry-driven representations. However, they largely ignore tongue dynamics, leading to anatomically inconsistent mouth interiors during speech and expressive motions. We introduce the first framework for cross-identity tongue dynamics transfer in face reenactment. We propose a foundation-model-assisted bootstrapping pipeline that produces a dedicated tongue segmentation model for in-the-wild reenactment without curated annotations. We further introduce a spatially constrained latent masked diffusion model for realistic tongue synthesis, with adaptive mask dilation for seamless mouth boundary transitions. Extensive experiments demonstrate improvements of more than two times over all baselines on every tongue-specific metric. We additionally propose a VLM-based evaluation protocol that replicates expert annotation at scale, confirming perceptual superiority across all ablation variants.

</details>


### [58] [Temporal Concentration from Rollout Errors: Implicit Preference Optimization for Text-to-Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28058)
*Henglin Liu,Fangyuan Kong,Jing Wang,Yizhou Lin,Nisha Huang,Chang Liu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“集中隐式偏好优化”的后训练框架，通过从去噪过程中直接生成隐式偏好信号，并利用帧级差异定位失败片段，将优化集中于这些高错误区域，从而有效解决了视频生成中的时空伪影问题，显著提升了生成视频的真实感和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于直接偏好优化的扩散模型视频生成方法在提升视觉质量的同时，仍存在运动崩溃、物体闪烁和颜色过饱和等时间稀疏伪影，严重阻碍了感知真实感。现有方法面临两大关键限制：1）偏好归因瓶颈，即离线人工标注成本高昂且无法捕捉学习动态，在线奖励信号虽具感知性但不稳定且有偏；2）时间信用分配错误，即均匀应用的监督无法有效针对伪影发生的短暂片段。

Method: 本文提出了集中隐式偏好优化框架，用于视频扩散模型的后训练。其核心方法是：1）给定真实视频，模型为其添加前向噪声并通过迭代去噪进行重建，将原始视频视为优选样本，重建视频视为非优选样本，从而直接从去噪过程中导出隐式偏好信号，无需人工标注或外部奖励模型；2）通过计算原始视频与重建视频之间的帧级差异来定位失败发生的时间点，进而计算时间重建误差，并将优化集中于高误差片段，实现对易失败区域的精准修正。

Result: 大量实验表明，cIPO在多个数据集上一致地增强了视频的真实感和时间连贯性。

Conclusion: 提出的cIPO框架通过结合隐式偏好信号和时间集中优化，有效且高效地解决了视频生成中的时空伪影问题，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in preference alignment for diffusion-based video generation, particularly via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), have significantly improved visual quality. However, temporally sparse artifacts such as motion collapse, object flickering, and color oversaturation remain a major barrier to perceptual realism. Existing methods struggle with these issues due to two key limitations: (1) the preference attribution bottleneck, where offline human annotations are costly and fail to accurately capture learning dynamics, while online reward signals are rollout-aware but often unstable and biased; and (2) temporal credit misallocation, where uniformly applied supervision cannot effectively target the brief segments in which artifacts occur. To address these challenges, we propose concentrated Implicit Preference Optimization (cIPO), a post-training framework for video diffusion models. cIPO derives implicit preference signals directly from the denoising process: given a real video, the model adds forward noise and reconstructs it via iterative denoising, treating the original as the preferred sample and the reconstruction as the dispreferred one. This formulation captures inference-time errors without requiring human annotations or external reward models. Moreover, frame-level discrepancies between original and reconstructed videos reveal when failures occur. cIPO leverages this by computing temporal reconstruction errors and concentrating optimization on high-error segments, enabling more precise correction of failure-prone regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that cIPO consistently enhances video authenticity and temporal coherence across multiple datasets, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of implicit preference with temporally concentrated optimization.

</details>


### [59] [Landmark shape spaces with induced metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28064)
*Sarang Joshi,Peter W. Michor,Stefan Sommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文统一了两种形状空间方法：Kendall的标记形状空间（在欧几里得几何中去除刚性运动并固定尺度）和基于微分同胚群右不变Sobolev度量的标记配置空间。新构建的形状空间兼具两者优点：度量的正则性防止了标记点碰撞，度量独立于标记点数量定义，保留局部刚性变换，移除全局刚性运动并固定尺度。为此，文章引入了一个特殊的Sobolev型算子——屏蔽弹性算子，并展示了其如何下推以实现所需几何，同时提供了数值求解匹配问题和计算测地线的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kendall形状空间通过去除刚性运动和固定尺度来定义形状，但其几何（如Procrustes度量）可能在标记点密集或数量变化时表现不佳。另一方面，基于微分同胚群的度量框架能提供正则性（防止标记点碰撞）且度量定义独立于标记点数量，但通常不保持刚性运动不变。本文旨在统一这两种方法，构建一个既保持形状不变性（刚性运动、尺度）又具备良好正则性和可扩展性的新形状空间。

Method: 1. 定义了一个特殊的Sobolev型算子——“屏蔽弹性算子”。该算子的关键性质是其零空间精确地由刚性运动构成。2. 展示了如何从该算子诱导的度量（在微分同胚群上）下推（descend）到标记配置空间，从而得到一个新的Riemannian度量。这个下推的度量在配置空间中保留了局部刚性变换不变性，并移除了全局刚性运动、固定了尺度。3. 提出了数值方法来解决在此新几何下的匹配问题（如模板到目标的匹配）并计算测地线。

Result: 成功构建了一类新的标记形状空间。这些空间具有以下特性：a) 度量的正则性（源于Sobolev度量）有效防止了标记点之间的碰撞；b) 度量在欧几里得空间中定义，不依赖于标记点的具体数量，便于处理可变或大量标记点；c) 保留了局部刚性变换不变性，同时严格移除了全局刚性运动和尺度因子；d) 提供了数值计算工具（用于匹配和测地线计算），使得新框架能够应用于实际问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入屏蔽弹性算子并构建其下推度量，成功地统一了Kendall形状空间的形状不变性与基于Sobolev度量的标记配置空间的正则性。新的形状空间克服了各自原有方法的局限，为形状分析提供了一个几何性质更优、适用范围更广的数学框架，并展示了其数值可计算性。

Abstract: We present a unification of Kendall's landmark shape spaces, where rigid motions are factored out and scale fixed on landmark configurations equipped with Euclidean geometry, with landmark configuration spaces carrying Riemannian metrics descending from right-invariant Sobolev metrics on the diffeomorphism group. The resulting new landmark shape spaces achieve the defining properties of both approaches: The regularity of the descending metric prevents landmarks from colliding, the metric is defined in the ambient space independent of the number of landmarks, local rigid transformations are preserved, global rigid motions are removed, and scale fixed. To achieve this, we define a particular Sobolev-type operator, the screened elasticity operator, whose null-space consists exactly of the rigid motions, we show how this operator descends to achieve the desired geometry, and we present approaches to solving matching problems and computing geodesics numerically. The resulting construction allows the use of landmark configuration spaces with sufficiently regular metrics in applications while retaining the shape invariances that are a hallmark of Kendall's shape spaces.

</details>


### [60] [BladeYOLO: Wind Turbine Blade Defect Detection with Limited Annotations and Weak-Saliency Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28065)
*Yabin Xu,Fangtao Zhang,Fan Wang,Zhan Wang,Honghua Chen,Mingqiang Wei,Haoran Xie,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对风力涡轮机叶片缺陷检测在真实场景中数据有限、缺陷微小且难以识别的挑战，本文提出了BladeYOLO框架。该框架通过整合预训练的Vision Transformer与YOLO、创新的弱缺陷增强模块以及风格注入模块，显著提升了在有限数据下的检测精度与鲁棒性，并在多个数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风力涡轮机叶片缺陷的现场检测极具挑战，主要源于：1) 现场标注数据有限；2) 缺陷本身规模小、对比度低、与复杂背景难以区分。这些因素严重限制了现有检测器的鲁棒性和实际应用效果。因此，亟需一种能够在有限监督下有效捕捉微小、低对比度缺陷特征并适应环境变化的检测方法。

Method: 本文提出了BladeYOLO框架，其核心方法包括三个创新组件：1) **骨干网络集成**：将使用DINOv3自监督预训练权重初始化的Vision Transformer (ViT) 骨干集成到YOLOv12-L中，以迁移大规模通用视觉先验知识，提升在有限标注下的特征表征能力。2) **Mamba引导的弱缺陷增强模块**：该模块包含一个用于保留高频结构细节的**细节增强多尺度分支**，以及一个用于将高层语义信息渐进传播至浅层特征的**跨Mamba模块**，从而增强对微小缺陷的感知。3) **轻量级风格注入器模块**：通过傅里叶分解捕获与环境相关的风格信息，并将其注入ViT的特定自注意力层，以提升模型对环境引起外观变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 在WTBlade-Defect数据集上的大量实验表明，BladeYOLO取得了优越的性能。进一步的标注预算实验显示，该方法在训练标注减少的情况下仍能保持良好性能。在公开的Wind Surface Defect数据集上的评估，进一步证实了BladeYOLO的跨数据集鲁棒性。**具体而言，在该公开数据集上，BladeYOLO在mAP$_{50}$指标上超越最佳对比方法3.5%，在mAP$_{50-95}$指标上超越2.5%。**

Conclusion: BladeYOLO通过融合预训练视觉Transformer、创新的弱缺陷增强机制和鲁棒的风格注入策略，有效解决了风力涡轮机叶片缺陷检测中数据稀缺和缺陷微小难辨的挑战。该框架不仅在目标数据集上表现优异，还展现出良好的泛化能力，为工业场景下的缺陷检测提供了一种高效、鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Wind turbine blade defect detection remains highly challenging in real-world inspection scenarios due to limited on-site data and the subtle visual characteristics of defects. In practice, blade defects are often small-scale, low-contrast, and difficult to distinguish from complex backgrounds, which significantly limits the robustness of existing detectors. To address these challenges, we propose BladeYOLO, a defect detection framework for wind turbine blades. Specifically, we integrate a Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone initialized with DINOv3 self-supervised pre-trained weights into YOLOv12-L, enabling the transfer of large-scale generic visual priors to blade defect detection and improving feature representation under limited training annotations. To enhance the perception of subtle defects, we further develop a Mamba-guided Weak-Defect Enhancement module, which consists of a Detail-Enhanced Multi-scale Branch for preserving high-frequency structural cues and a Cross-Mamba module for progressively propagating high-level semantic guidance to shallow features. In addition, we introduce a lightweight Style-Injector module that captures environment-related style information via Fourier decomposition and injects it into selected ViT self-attention layers, thereby improving robustness against environment-induced appearance variations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BladeYOLO achieves superior performance on the WTBlade-Defect dataset, with additional annotation-budget experiments showing its favorable performance under reduced training annotations. Evaluation on the public Wind Surface Defect dataset further provides supportive evidence for the cross-dataset robustness of BladeYOLO. In particular, on this public dataset, BladeYOLO outperforms the best competing method by 3.5\% in mAP$_{50}$ and 2.5\% in mAP$_{50-95}$.

</details>


### [61] [mmRadarTwin: A Measurement-Calibrated Signal-Level Digital Twin Platform for Indoor mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28108)
*Jianyi Zhou,Chenghao Zhang,Yanli Li,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了mmRadarTwin，一个用于室内毫米波雷达的信号级、路径归因数字孪生平台，旨在解决室内雷达感知难以复现的问题，并提供了一种构建、比较和诊断雷达数字孪生的实用工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内毫米波雷达的感知结果（如距离-角度响应）受场景几何、材料、多径效应、硬件和信号处理等多种因素影响，难以复现。现有的数字孪生或仿真工具通常提供渲染、信道或路径级别的数据，而雷达传感需要能在与真实FMCW测量相同的信号域中进行处理和比较的复杂信号产品，因此需要一个能填补这一差距的工具。

Method: 作者提出了mmRadarTwin平台，它通过一个共享的接收信道和距离-角度处理接口，将真实的雷达测量分支与基于虚幻引擎（Unreal Engine）的场景仿真分支连接起来。仿真器生成复杂的多通道接收网格，并导出详细的路径贡献记录，识别每个仿真回波的参与者、材料标签、传播事件和支持的输出单元。平台在办公室环境中使用商用单站毫米波雷达和移动场景捕获硬件进行了评估。

Result: 在22个雷达位置、154个测量姿态的评估中，当前仅基于物理路径的仿真器在中央可用视场内，能回忆（recall）70.8%的测量活跃且由几何支持的响应区域。同时，仿真结果揭示了由于弱/缺失路径支持、响应偏移、不支持的锚点以及缺失物理机制导致的残差（residuals）。

Conclusion: mmRadarTwin并非旨在实现完全的雷达图重建或跨场景泛化，而是作为一个实用的系统工作流程，为室内毫米波雷达数字孪生的构建、比较和诊断提供了方法和工具。

Abstract: Indoor mmWave radar perception is difficult to reproduce because measured range-angle responses depend on scene geometry, material response, multipath, hardware conventions, and signal processing. Existing ray-tracing and digital-twin tools often expose rendering, channel, or path-level quantities, while radar sensing requires complex signal products that can be processed and compared in the same domain as real FMCW measurements. We present mmRadarTwin, a signal-level and path-attributed digital-twin platform for indoor mmWave radar. mmRadarTwin links a real radar measurement branch with an Unreal Engine scene-simulation branch through a shared receive-channel and range-angle processing interface. The simulator writes complex multi-channel receive grids and exports per-path contribution records that identify the actor, material tag, propagation event, and output-bin support of each simulated return. We evaluate mmRadarTwin in an office deployment using a commodity monostatic mmWave radar and mobile scene-capture hardware. Across 154 measured poses spanning 22 radar locations, the current physics-only path-basis simulator recalls 70.8% of measurement-active geometry-supported response regions in the central usable field of view while exposing residuals caused by weak or missing path support, shifted responses, unsupported anchors, and missing physical mechanisms. Rather than claiming complete radar-map reconstruction or cross-room generalization, mmRadarTwin establishes a practical systems workflow for constructing, comparing, and diagnosing indoor radar digital twins.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Practical Algorithm Selection for Unsupervised Domain Adaptation in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28125)
*Yiheng Xiong,Luisa Gallée,Daniel Santak Wolf,Heiko Hillenhagen,Michael Götz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无标签的选择标准，用于在临床部署中联合选择最佳的无监督域适应算法及其超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在众多无监督域适应算法，但在临床实践中，由于目标域缺乏标签，无法直接评估，因此难以选择最合适的算法和恰当的超参数。

Method: 该方法从多个算法和不同超参数训练的候选模型池出发，通过构建一个无需目标标签的“一致性参考”来为每个候选模型打分，并选择得分最高者。该参考的构建分两步：首先，利用多个无标签选择信号，为每种算法提名一个模型；其次，跨算法聚合这些提名模型，为每个无标签目标样本生成一个参考预测。选择其预测与该参考最一致的候选模型进行部署。

Result: 在四个脑部MRI和四个胸部X光数据集的七个临床相关迁移场景中进行的实验表明，该方法比其他方法取得了更好的选择性能，并且在不同的算法池中均保持有效。

Conclusion: 该方法朝着在临床实践中为无监督域适应部署进行实用的、无标签的算法选择迈出了一步。

Abstract: Numerous unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) algori-thms exist, but for clinical practice, selecting the best-suited one along with proper hyperparameters often remains unclear, as the unlabeled deployment (target) domain prevents direct evaluation. We propose a label-free criterion that jointly selects the algorithm and hyperparameters for UDA. Given a pool of candidate models from multiple algorithms trained with different hyperparameters, our approach scores each candidate against an agreement reference, and selects the one with the highest score. The agreement reference is constructed in two levels without using target labels. First, we leverage multiple label-free selection signals, using each to nominate a model within every algorithm. Second, the nominated models are aggregated across algorithms to form a reference prediction for each unlabeled target sample. The candidate whose predictions agree most with this reference is then selected for deployment. Experimental results on four brain MRI and four chest X-ray datasets across seven clinically relevant transfer scenarios show that our method achieves better selection performance than other methods and remains effective across different algorithm pools. Our approach takes a step towards practical, label-free algorithm selection for clinical deployment of UDA.

</details>


### [63] [Face and Voice Cross-modal Association with Learning Convex Feature Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28129)
*Taewan Kim,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单而强大的跨模态特征嵌入方法，用于人脸与语音的关联，有效解决了音视频模态间的异质性问题，减少了误匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态关联方法虽然研究了语音片段与面部图像的相关性，但低估了处理音频和视频之间模态间特征异质性的重要性，导致了大量假阳性和假阴性结果。本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 提出的方法通过在跨模态特征之间学习一个中间特征（使同一人的语音和面部特征嵌入到一个凸包中）来学习跨模态特征嵌入。此外，结合跨模态注意力机制与凸包嵌入技术，通过最小化类间差异来有效减弱假阳性和假阴性。

Result: 在大规模VoxCeleb数据集上，对跨模态验证、匹配和检索任务进行了全面评估。大量实验结果表明，该方法相比现有最先进的方法取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于凸包嵌入和跨模态注意力机制的跨模态特征嵌入方法，有效提升了人脸与语音关联任务的性能，减少了误匹配，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Face-and-voice association learning is one of the most challenging tasks in deep learning. In this paper, we propose a simple but powerful cross-modal feature embedding method for the association of faces and voices. Previous work has studied cross-modal association tasks to establish the correlation between voice clips and facial images. These works have addressed cross-modal discrimination but underestimate the importance of handling heterogeneity in inter-modal features between audio and video, resulting in a lot of false positives and false negatives. To tackle the problem, the proposed method learns the embeddings of cross-modal features by making another feature exist between cross-modal features, facilitating the voice and face features of the same person to be embedded in a convex hull. Moreover, the incorporation of cross-modal attention mechanisms with convex embedding techniques represents a highly effective strategy for the attenuation of false positives and false negatives, accomplished via the minimization of inter-class discrepancies. We exhaustively evaluated our method for cross-modal verification, matching, and retrieval tasks on the large-scale VoxCeleb dataset. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves notable improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [64] [Collaborative Feature Aggregation for Face Super-Resolution and Robust Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28130)
*Juheon Hwang,Taewan Kim,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Transformer的协作方法，利用多视角/多序列面部图像进行人脸超分辨率和行人重识别，通过联合身份重建和渐进式图像恢复提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人脸超分辨率（SR）方法在低分辨率图像中存在模糊和失真问题，即便是使用面部关键点或分割的图像/视频SR方法也面临类似挑战。因此，需要一种能够利用多个相关面部观察（跨时间或视角）来准确估计共同面部特征的方法。

Method: 1. 提出基于Transformer的协作特征聚合方法，以统一来自多序列或多视角数据的身份特征。2. 设计一个级联SR网络，逐步恢复高分辨率人脸图像并实现面部特征的渐进式统一。3. 将统一后的身份表示应用于行人重识别（ReID）场景，实现退化图像下的准确匹配。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，该方法在人脸超分辨率和行人重识别任务上均优于其他最先进的方法，性能提升一致。

Conclusion: 联合身份重建与从多面部输入中进行渐进式图像恢复，能有效增强下游视觉识别任务的性能。

Abstract: We propose a novel collaborative approach for face super-resolution (SR) and robust person re-identification from sequential or multi-view facial images. Traditional SR methods often suffer from blurring and distortion in faces recovered from poor-quality images due to low resolution. Image- and video-based facial SR methods using facial landmarks or segmentation also have similar challenges. To overcome these limitations, we leverage multiple correlated facial observations, across time or viewpoints, by introducing a transformer-based collaborative feature aggregation method that unifies identity features from multi-sequence or multi-view data. This allows faces in multiple sequences of an individual to contribute to accurately estimating common facial features. Furthermore, we propose a cascade SR network to progressively restore the high-resolution image of the target's face with gradual facial feature unification. The unified identity representation is further utilized in person re-identification scenarios, enabling accurate matching even under severe image degradation. The exhaustive experimental results and comparisons show that our method outperforms other state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating consistent improvements in both face super-resolution and re-identification performance. Our work highlights the effectiveness of joint identity reconstruction and progressive image restoration from multiple facial inputs in enhancing downstream visual recognition tasks.

</details>


### [65] [Convolutional Neural Shading for High-Quality 3D Reconstruction from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28132)
*Juheon Hwang,Taewan Kim,Heeseok Oh,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出卷积神经着色（CNS），一种用于从多视图图像重建高质量3D形状的新流程。该方法通过卷积神经着色器捕获黑暗和无纹理区域的几何变化，并结合精细细节位移网络减少图像边界处的表面不规则性，实验结果表明其在几何精度和渲染质量上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于神经辐射场等可微渲染的方法依赖于单点几何信息（如位置和法线），导致在黑暗和无纹理区域缺乏详细的局部几何细节。为了解决这一局限性，需要一种能捕获更丰富空间变化信息的方法，以提升几何重建的精度和表面细节。

Method: 提出卷积神经着色（CNS）流程：1) 使用卷积神经着色器，通过卷积操作捕捉邻域内的像素和几何变化，即使在黑暗或无纹理区域也能提取有效信息；2) 引入精细细节位移网络，利用表面几何的空间信息，在渲染坐标中关联相邻值，学习精细的位移细节，以修正图像边界处的表面不规则性。

Result: 通过大量实验验证，CNS在重建的3D形状质量和渲染图像质量上均显著优于现有最先进方法。具体表现为在几何细节还原、表面平滑度以及边界区域的视觉表现上有明显提升。

Conclusion: 卷积神经着色（CNS）通过结合卷积神经着色器和精细细节位移网络，有效克服了传统单点几何信息的局限性，实现了从多视图图像到高质量3D形状的精确重建，为神经渲染和3D重建领域提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: We propose a convolutional neural shading (CNS), a novel pipeline to reconstruct high-quality 3D shapes from multi-view images. Several recent studies have used neural radiance fields and other neural differentiable rendering methods to understand 3D geometry. However, these approaches rely on single-point geometric information, such as positions and normals of the surface, leading to a lack of detailed local geometry. Our approach addresses the inherent limitations of single-point information by leveraging a neural shader to capture variations even in dark and textureless regions with a convolutional neural shader, resulting in far more accurate geometry predictions. Additionally, our method mitigates surface irregularities at image boundaries by introducing a fine-detail displacement network, which utilizes spatial information of surface geometry and learns fine displacement details by correlating neighboring values in the rendering coordinates. Through extensive experiments, our proposed method has demonstrated significant quality improvements in the reconstructed shapes and rendered images over current state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [66] [What Makes Deep Learning Work for Traditional Chinese Medicine Tongue Diagnosis? A Comprehensive Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28148)
*Longxia Gao,Linan Wang,Yuhe Han,Junze Geng,Meng Zhang,Hanqing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一项系统性消融研究探索了深度学习在中医舌诊自动化中的应用设计空间，提出了六个关键设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在中医舌诊自动化方面展现出潜力，但其设计空间尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 在TongueDx2数据集（5109张图像，其中976张为专家标注）和合并的11101个样本数据集上，进行严格的5折交叉验证下的系统性消融研究，比较了六种骨干架构、四种损失函数、五种数据增强策略和六种训练策略。

Result: 最佳的976样本模型（ConvNeXt-Tiny，采用受控增强和弱组集成）加权F1分数达到0.6625；最佳的11101样本模型加权F1分数达到0.7761。研究得出了六个关键设计原则：1) ConvNeXt-Tiny参数效率最优；2) BCE损失函数比Asymmetric Loss显著优越（提升2.7%）；3) 受控色彩增强至关重要；4) 弱组集成替换（提升2.1%）优于概率平均；5) 数据扩展带来20.6%的性能提升；6) 标签维度从13扩展到45会导致灾难性崩溃（F1从0.78降至0.22）。

Conclusion: 这些设计原则对于处理类别不平衡的多标签医学图像分类任务具有普适性。

Abstract: Deep learning has shown promise for automated tongue diagnosis in traditional Chinese medicine (TCM), yet the design space remains underexplored. We conducted a systematic ablation study spanning 20+ model versions under rigorous 5-fold cross-validation on TongueDx2 (5,109 images, 976 expert-annotated) and a merged dataset of 11,101 samples. We compared six backbone architectures, four loss functions, five augmentation strategies, and six training strategies. The best 976-sample model achieved weighted-F1 of 0.6625 using ConvNeXt-Tiny with restrained augmentation and weak-group ensemble, while the best 11,101-sample model reached weighted-F1 of 0.7761. Six key design principles emerged: (1) ConvNeXt-Tiny offers optimal parameter efficiency; (2) BCE substantially outperforms Asymmetric Loss (+2.7%); (3) restrained color augmentation is critical; (4) weak-group ensemble replacement (+2.1%) outperforms probability averaging; (5) data scaling yielded +20.6% improvement; (6) expanding from 13 to 45 label dimensions caused catastrophic collapse (0.78 to 0.22). These principles are generalizable to multi-label medical image classification with class imbalance.

</details>


### [67] [OPLD: On-Policy Latent Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28154)
*Shoutai Zhu,Tianyang Xu,Bin Sun,Mingyuan Xu,Yu Liu,Qinzhen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OPLD（On-Policy Latent Distillation）的简单框架，旨在将特权多模态链式思考诱导的推理能力迁移至潜变量推理表征中，从而改善视觉推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态链式思考方法受限于外部定义的推理轨迹和视觉操作，限制了其发展灵活、抽象的视觉思维能力。虽然基于潜变量的推理提供了一个有前景的方向，但当前方法主要通过监督潜变量状态与压缩辅助视觉特征的对齐来实现，将其视为视觉观测的代理而非主动推理状态，因而未能完全内化多模态链式思考所诱导的抽象推理过程。

Method: 提出OPLD（On-Policy Latent Distillation）框架。该框架的核心思想是将特权多模态链式思考诱导的推理能力，通过知识蒸馏的方式转移到模型的潜变量推理表征中。这不同于常规的基于特征对齐的方法，它实现了在推理过程层面对潜变量表征的监督。

Result: 在多个多模态基准测试上进行的广泛实验表明，OPLD持续优于现有的潜变量推理方法，并在多个基准上实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在推理过程层面对潜变量表征进行监督，为多模态潜变量推理提供了一种比传统特征级对齐更有效的范式。

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal Chain-of-Thought (CoT) improves visual reasoning by incorporating auxiliary visual evidence into intermediate reasoning. However, existing approaches remain constrained by externally defined reasoning traces and visual operations, limiting their ability to develop flexible and abstract visual thinking. Reasoning with latent has recently offered a promising direction by internalizing intermediate computation into continuous representations. Nevertheless, existing visual-latent methods mainly supervise latent states through alignment with compressed auxiliary visual features, treating them as proxies for visual observations rather than active reasoning states. Consequently, they capture the provided evidence but fail to fully internalize the abstract reasoning process induced by multimodal CoT. In this paper, we propose OPLD (On-Policy Latent Distillation), a simple framework that transfers the reasoning capability induced by privileged multimodal CoT into latent reasoning representations. Extensive experiments on diverse multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that OPLD consistently outperforms existing latent reasoning methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks. The results suggest that supervising latent representations at the reasoning-process level provides a more effective paradigm for multimodal latent reasoning than conventional feature-level alignment.

</details>


### [68] [S-Avatar: Diffusion-Guided Gaussian Head Avatars from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28164)
*Hail Song,Seokhwan Yang,Jiwon Yang,Woojin Cho,Woontack Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We propose S-Avatar, a novel method for generating photorealistic 3D head avatars from a single image using a diffusion-guided 3D model generation module and strategies for animating 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). While single-image head avatar reconstruction is crucial for lifelike Virtual Reality (VR) applications, existing approaches often struggle to preserve 3D consistency under unseen viewpoints. S-Avatar addresses this limitation through a three-stage pipeline. First, a high-resolution 3DGS is synthesized directly from a single image using a diffusion-based Gaussian splat generation module. Next, the parametric head model FLAME is aligned with the generated 3DGS by optimizing its parameters and spatial transformations. Finally, to adapt the 3DGS to FLAME variations, we construct a binding template that encodes the spatial relationship between the initial splats and FLAME. The dynamic 3D head avatar can then be rendered in real time by deforming the 3DGS with the binding template. By combining diffusion-guided canonical 3DGS generation with FLAME-based control, our method achieves efficient and accurate reconstruction with enhanced 3D consistency. Evaluations on public datasets demonstrate that S-Avatar outperforms state-of-the-art methods in novel-view and expression generation, achieving superior realism and consistency. Consequently, our approach represents a significant advance in accessible avatar creation, applicable to a wide range of VR/AR applications. The project page is available at https://github.com/hailsong/savatar.

</details>


### [69] [Think with Extra-Image: A Farmland Segmentation Agent Driven by Spatio-Temporal Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28186)
*Haiyang Wu,Weiliang Mu,Zhuofei Du,Dandan Zhong,Kaijie Shi,Haifeng Li,Chao Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有农田遥感图像分割方法基于单幅图像内特征假设，但农田外观易受物候和背景干扰，导致歧义。作者提出FarmSeeker智能体，通过动态识别模糊区域并按需查询外部时空信息来优化分割，并构建了首个全球尺度基准GSFS-Bench进行评估。实验表明其性能更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农田遥感图像分割遵循“图像内思考”范式，假设当前图像包含足够视觉证据。然而，农田外观随物候和空间背景变化，且易与其他地物混淆，导致单幅图像观测信息不足。分割歧义的根本原因在于所需时空信息超出当前图像范围，因此需要引入额外信息来提升可靠性。

Method: 作者从信息瓶颈视角将农田遥感图像分割重新定义为由任务相关额外时空信息增益驱动的动态决策过程。基于此，提出FarmSeeker智能体，该智能体能识别图像中的模糊区域，推断其成因，并按需查询外部时空信息以辅助分割。此外，构建了首个支持推理-查询的全球尺度高分辨率农田遥感图像分割基准GSFS-Bench。

Result: 实验表明，FarmSeeker智能体在GSFS-Bench基准上实现了比现有方法更稳定的分割性能。该智能体通过动态查询外部信息有效处理了分割歧义问题，验证了其设计有效性。

Conclusion: 农田遥感图像分割需超越单幅图像局限，引入外部时空信息。FarmSeeker智能体通过动态识别模糊区域并按需查询信息，显著提升了分割稳定性。所构建的GSFS-Bench为相关研究提供了首个全球尺度高分辨率基准，推动了领域发展。

Abstract: Existing farmland remote sensing image (FRSI) segmentation follows a "Think with Intra-Image" paradigm, assuming that the current image contains sufficient visual evidence for reliable segmentation. Yet farmland appearance varies with phenology and spatial context and is often confused with other land-cover, making instantaneous, local observations inadequate. Thus, segmentation ambiguity stems not only from limited model representation, but more fundamentally from the required spatio-temporal information lying beyond the current image. Based on this insight, we redefine FRSI segmentation from an information bottleneck perspective as a dynamic decision process driven by task-relevant extra spatio-temporal information gain. We further propose FarmSeeker, a dynamic FRSI segmentation agent that identifies ambiguous regions, reasons about their causes, and queries extra spatio-temporal information on demand for accurate segmentation. To evaluate FarmSeeker, we construct GSFS-Bench, the first global-scale, high-resolution FRSI segmentation benchmark that supports reasoning-querying. Experiments show that FarmSeeker achieves more stable segmentation performance than existing methods. The project is publicly available at: https://withoutocean.github.io/FarmSeeker/

</details>


### [70] [Scaling Vision-Language Models Is Not Enough to Mitigate Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28211)
*Ioannis Sarridis,Ioannis Kompatsiaris,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次对194个公开的视觉语言模型（VLMs）进行了大规模实证研究，发现模型规模与鲁棒性（在应对虚假相关性时）的关联性随着评估任务的复杂化而减弱，而训练数据的属性（规模与质量）则对模型的鲁棒性有更一致的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以CLIP为代表的视觉语言模型（VLMs）是多模态系统的基础，但其对于虚假相关性的鲁棒性在大规模上仍未得到充分理解。

Method: 研究对194个公开可用的VLMs（涵盖16个模型家族、多种模型规模、24个训练数据集）进行了大规模实证评估，使用了三个评估基准：ImageNet（整体性能）、CelebA（典型单属性偏差）和UrbanCars（复杂的多属性偏差）。

Result: 研究发现，模型规模与性能之间的Spearman相关性随评估任务的变化而显著减弱：在ImageNet上ρ=0.68，在单属性偏差基准上ρ=0.48，在多属性偏差基准上ρ=0.05。相比之下，训练数据的属性（规模和质量）与最差群体准确率在两个偏差基准上都表现出更一致的关联。值得注意的是，在可比规模下，经过策划的数据集相比未策划的数据集能带来高达25%的性能提升。此外，架构选择（例如，图像块大小、图像分辨率）的影响具有高度的上下文依赖性，随基准特性（包括偏差类型及其在图像中的空间分布）而变化。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型对虚假相关性的鲁棒性不单纯取决于模型规模，而与训练数据的质量和数量更为相关。架构设计的影响则高度依赖于具体的评估场景和偏差类型。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP are now foundational to multimodal systems, yet their robustness to spurious correlations remains poorly understood at scale. We present the first large-scale empirical study of 194 publicly available VLMs, including 16 model families, covering a wide range of model sizes, 24 training datasets, and three evaluation benchmarks, namely ImageNet (overall performance), CelebA (typical single-attribute bias), and UrbanCars (complex multi-attribute biases). Across these settings, the Spearman correlation between model scale and performance weakens as evaluation shifts from ImageNet ($ρ{=}0.68$) to single-attribute ($ρ{=}0.48$) and further to multi-attribute ($ρ{=}0.05$) bias benchmarks. In contrast, properties of the training data (size and quality) show more consistent relationships with worst-group accuracy across both bias benchmarks. Notably, curated datasets yield improvements of up to 25% over uncurated alternatives at a comparable scale. Finally, the effect of architectural choices (e.g., patch size, image resolution) is highly context-dependent, varying with the nature of the benchmark, including the type of bias and its spatial distribution within images.

</details>


### [71] [FaithEyes: Towards Faithful Tool Use via Multi-Agent Process-Image Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28225)
*Haoqing Wang,Xingrun Xing,Wei Xia,Ziheng Li,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对智能视觉语言模型工具调用不忠实的问题（即模型常调用无关工具但仍能正确回答），本文提出了FaithEyes多智能体自评判框架，通过评判工具调用的有用性来抑制奖励欺骗，显著提升了工具使用的忠实度与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能视觉语言模型（Agentic VLMs）通过交替文本推理与工具调用（如裁剪、代码图像操作）进行多模态推理，但近期研究发现模型常进行“不忠实”的工具调用——处理了与问题无关的图像区域，却仍获得正确答案。这表明模型依赖先验知识或原始图像而非工具获取的证据，浪费计算且不可靠。现有方法的主要局限在于：1）工具奖励无法区分有用与无用的工具调用；2）工具反馈缺乏有用性信号，导致奖励欺骗。

Method: 本文提出FaithEyes，一个基于多智能体自评判的框架。核心方法包括：1）使用VLM作为评判者，判断每个处理后的图像是否有助于回答问题；2）将评判结果注入推理上下文，作为工具观测的一部分以指导后续推理；3）利用“有用工具比率”缩放工具奖励，以抑制奖励欺骗；4）设计多智能体框架，使模型自身作为子智能体在推理时评判主智能体的工具调用，确保训练与测试的一致性，无需外部模型依赖。训练采用基于开源数据的两阶段流程：监督微调（SFT）后进行强化学习（RL）。

Result: 通过两阶段SFT+RL训练后，FaithEyes在视觉感知与推理基准测试上取得了具有竞争力或更优的准确性，同时显著提高了工具调用的忠实性。

Conclusion: FaithEyes通过引入工具有用性评判机制并将其整合到训练与推理流程中，有效解决了智能VLMs工具调用不忠实的问题，提升了模型推理的可靠性与可解释性。该框架展示了自评判机制在强化学习中抑制奖励欺骗、引导模型进行更可信工具使用的潜力。

Abstract: Agentic vision-language models (VLMs), which interleave textual reasoning with explicit tool calls such as cropping and code-based image manipulation, have emerged as a compelling paradigm for reliable and interpretable multimodal reasoning. However, recent studies have revealed that such models often use tools unfaithfully. Many process images are irrelevant to the question (e.g., the tool crops the wrong region or misses the queried target), yet the call still receives full credit and the model still answers correctly. Such decorative or misaligned tool calls waste computation and reveal that the model leans on prior knowledge or the original image rather than the evidence it retrieves. This may stem from two limitations of prevailing methods: the tool reward fails to distinguish useful from useless calls, and tool feedback carries no signal of usefulness. To this end, we introduce FaithEyes, a multi-agent self-judging framework. Concretely, we use a VLM to judge whether each process image helps answer the question. The judgement is injected into the reasoning context as part of the tool observation to help subsequent reasoning, and meanwhile is used to scale the tool reward by the helpful-tool ratio to suppress reward hacking. To keep judgement available at evaluation and thus ensure train-test consistency, we further design a multi-agent framework where the model itself serves as a subagent to judge the tool calls from main agent, eliminating any dependence on an external model at inference. Training via a two-stage SFT + RL pipeline on adapted open-source data, FaithEyes attains competitive or superior accuracy across visual perception and reasoning benchmarks, while markedly improving tool faithfulness. The homepage is at https://github.com/Mosi-AI/FaithEyes.

</details>


### [72] [EgoGenesis: Egocentric World-Action Modeling with Online Anchored Projective Memory and Action-3D RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28243)
*Zexuan Yan,Yuzhou Wu,Yue Ma,Zonghang He,Kaibo Yin,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Shijian Wang,Jinghong Liu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为Egocentric世界-动作模拟器的自我中心世界-动作模拟器，它能够合成可控、高质量的操作视频，以扩充稀缺的真实世界训练数据，从而提升具身AI的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收集多样化的自我中心视角数据成本高昂，这限制了具身AI的发展。为了扩充稀缺的真实世界训练数据，需要一种能够合成可控、高质量操作视频的方法。

Method: 该方法基于预训练的视频生成先验，并引入两个几何感知条件机制：1. 在线锚定投影记忆，它在自回归生成期间保留第一帧的3D场景锚点，同时定期刷新最近状态。2. 动作3D旋转位置嵌入，它使用相机感知的3D旋转坐标对末端执行器运动进行编码，并将动作几何注入骨架到视频的交叉注意力中，以实现精确控制。

Result: 这些组件提高了视觉保真度、几何稳定性和动作对齐，特别是在长序列的自我中心视角生成中。使用400条生成轨迹增强400条真实轨迹后，真实机器人在单臂任务上的分布外成功率从77%提升至84%，在双臂任务上从53%提升至70%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的自我中心世界-动作模拟器能够有效合成高质量的训练数据，显著提升了真实机器人在不同操作任务上的泛化能力，证明了合成数据在扩充训练数据集方面的价值。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers rich manipulation experience for embodied AI, yet collecting diverse egocentric data across scenes, objects, motions, and embodiments remains costly. We present \method, an egocentric world-action simulator that synthesizes controllable, high-quality manipulation videos to expand scarce real-world training data. \method{} builds on a pretrained video generation prior and introduces two geometry-aware conditioning mechanisms. Online Anchored Projective Memory (OAPM) preserves a first-frame 3D scene anchor while periodically refreshing a recent state during autoregressive generation. Action-3D Rotary Position Embedding (A3D-RoPE) encodes end-effector motion with camera-aware 3D rotary coordinates, injecting action geometry into skeleton-to-video cross-attention for precise control. Together, these components improve visual fidelity, geometric stability, and action alignment in long egocentric rollouts. Moreover, augmenting 400 real trajectories with 400 \method-generated trajectories improves out-of-distribution real-robot success from 77\% to 84\% on single-arm tasks and from 53\% to 70\% on dual-arm tasks, demonstrating that the synthesized data substantially improve downstream WAM generalization.

</details>


### [73] [Space2Ground 2.0: A Multi-Source Dataset and Framework for Agricultural Monitoring through Fusion of Street-Level and Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28247)
*Iason Tsardanidis,Alkiviadis Koukos,George Choumos,Vasileios Sitokontantinou,Charalampos Kontoes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Space2Ground 2.0框架，通过整合卫星时间序列与街景图像，构建了一个用于地块级农业监测的多源数据集和可复现方法，有效提升了作物分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确且可扩展的地块级农业监测仍然面临挑战，因为卫星对地观测仅能提供俯瞰视角，且光学观测常受云层影响而导致时间序列出现间隔。因此，需要引入能提供互补性信息的地面视角数据源来增强监测能力。

Method: 研究提出一个名为Space2Ground 2.0的多源框架，整合了Sentinel-1 SAR、Sentinel-2多光谱时间序列与通过Mapillary平台获取的地理标记街景图像。该框架包含一个自动化处理流程，执行语义过滤、图像质量评估、基于视点的地块关联以及数据集提炼，将海量众包图像转化为与地块关联、可用于分析的数据。

Result: 在塞浦路斯2022年生长季的应用中，该流程从超过90万张原始图像中筛选出46,050张标注的街景图像，并将它们与8,581个农业地块的卫星信息相关联。地块级作物分类实验表明，街景图像提供了互补的精细尺度视觉信息，将其与卫星时间序列集成时，能显著提升分类精度。

Conclusion: Space2Ground 2.0提供了一个公开可用的基准数据集和可复现的多模态农业监测方法论，该成果可应用于视觉验证、减少对昂贵实地检查的依赖以及数据驱动的农业政策实施。

Abstract: Accurate and scalable parcel-level agricultural monitoring remains challenging because satellite Earth Observation alone provides only an overhead perspective of agricultural parcels, while optical observations are further affected by cloud-induced temporal gaps. This paper presents Space2Ground 2.0, a multi-source framework integrating Sentinel-1 SAR and Sentinel-2 multispectral time series with geo-tagged street-level imagery acquired using vehicle-mounted cameras and shared through the Mapillary platform. A largely automated processing pipeline performs semantic filtering, image quality assessment, viewpoint-based parcel association, and dataset refinement, transforming large volumes of crowdsourced imagery into parcel-linked, analysis-ready data. Applied over Cyprus during the 2022 growing season, the pipeline produced a curated dataset of 46,050 annotated street-level images, selected from an initial collection exceeding 900,000 images and linked with satellite information for 8,581 agricultural parcels. The practical value of the dataset was assessed through parcel-level crop classification experiments using both single- and multi-source observations. The results demonstrate that street-level imagery provides complementary fine-scale visual information that enhances classification when integrated with satellite time series. Overall, Space2Ground 2.0 provides an openly available benchmark dataset and a reproducible methodology for multimodal agricultural monitoring, with potential applications in visual verification, reduced reliance on costly field inspections, and data-driven agricultural policy implementation.

</details>


### [74] [TARS: Timestep-Aware Data Scaling for 3D-Free Video Re-Shooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28261)
*Jiwen Liu,Shujuan Li,Xiaohan Li,Zijie Meng,Xinyue Liu,Yulong Xu,Yan Zhou,Guoxin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TARS，一种无需3D重建的视频重拍范式，通过文本驱动的语义视角规范（控制镜头规模、视角和第一/第三人称视角），实现对相机运动和视角的精确控制。该方法利用自监督训练学习相机动力学，能鲁棒地合成源视角外的区域，并支持反向角度重拍和视角切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频重拍方法依赖显式3D先验，受限于重建质量，在合成未见区域时表现不佳；或依赖稀缺的配对视频数据，阻碍了泛化能力。本文旨在通过文本驱动的语义控制，摆脱对3D重建和配对数据的依赖，实现更通用、可控的视频重拍。

Method: 1. 提出TARS：一种无需3D的视频重拍范式。2. 通过时间步敏感性分析，揭示相机运动主要在高噪声阶段（粗略时空结构形成时）建立。3. 设计自监督训练：在无配对数据或3D重建的情况下学习相机动态和基础视觉表征。4. 通过数据缩放和联合文本-相机条件注入，实现鲁棒的相机和视角控制。

Result: 实验证明，TARS比先前方法提供了更准确、时间一致性更好的相机控制。该方法能合理地合成在大幅度相机运动下源视角之外的区域，并成功实现了反向角度重拍和第一/第三人称视角切换。

Conclusion: TARS通过文本驱动的语义控制、对扩散过程中相机运动建立阶段的洞察以及自监督学习，成功实现了无需3D重建和配对数据的、鲁棒且可控的视频重拍，为相关任务提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Video re-shooting aims to regenerate videos with controllable camera motion and viewpoint. Existing methods rely on explicit 3D priors, which are limited by reconstruction quality and often perform poorly when synthesizing previously unseen regions, or on paired videos with different camera trajectories, whose scarcity hinders generalization. We revisit video re-shooting through text-driven semantic viewpoint specification, enabling control over shot scale, viewing angle, and first-/third-person perspective. To this end, we propose TARS, a 3D-free video re-shooting paradigm. Timestep-wise sensitivity analysis reveals that camera motion is primarily established during high-noise stages, where coarse spatiotemporal structures are formed. Based on this insight, we introduce self-supervised training to learn camera dynamics and fundamental visual representations without paired re-shooting data or 3D reconstruction. Through data scaling and joint textual-camera conditioning, TARS supports robust camera and viewpoint control, plausibly synthesizing regions beyond the source view under large camera motions while enabling reverse-angle re-shooting and perspective switching. Extensive experiments show that TARS provides more accurate and temporally consistent camera control than prior methods. Project Page: https://ymlinfeng.github.io/TARS.github.io/

</details>


### [75] [Theia: Large-Scale Multimodal Captioning and Automated Validation of the Incidents1M Dataset for Data-Free Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28269)
*Simone Giano,Lorenzo Severini,Alessandro Galdelli,Adriano Mancini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构建和验证用于灾难响应的大规模多模态数据集的新方法，通过生成高保真文本描述和引入图像盲LLM验证流程，显著提升了数据集的质量与可靠性，为跨模态知识蒸馏提供了可复现的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灾难管理领域的多模态数据集存在缺陷，如缺乏文本描述或文本-图像语义不对齐，这阻碍了视觉语言模型通过数据自由知识蒸馏进行有效的知识迁移。因此，需要一个高质量、语义对齐的多模态数据集来支持关键领域的应用。

Method: 从纯视觉的Incidents1M数据集出发，使用两种Qwen3.5架构（4B密集模型和35B MoE模型）为100,000张图像生成高保真文本描述。创新性地引入一个图像盲的LLM-as-a-Judge验证流程，利用Qwen3.5-9B模型，在不提供原始图像的情况下评估生成描述的质量，以模拟学生模型在数据自由蒸馏中遇到的模态差距。

Result: 在173,179个标签对上的评估显示，两种架构生成的描述具有高度语义一致性（78.65/100）。自动化评估揭示了生成的描述具有保守性，表现为高精确率（77.6%）和低召回率（46.0%）。这种特性最大限度地减少了错误的正例噪声，同时暴露了原始地面真实数据中的人工标注不一致性问题。

Conclusion: 本研究成功提供了一个可扩展的、经过LLM验证的多模态数据集，并建立了一个可复现的框架，这为推进跨模态知识蒸馏技术提供了重要支持，尤其在灾难响应等关键应用领域。

Abstract: The deployment of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in critical domains like disaster management requires high-quality multimodal datasets, especially for transferring knowledge via Data-Free Knowledge Distillation (DFKD). However, existing datasets in this domain either entirely lack descriptive text, such as Incidents1M, or suffer from severe text-image semantic misalignment, such as CrisisMMD. In this work, we present a novel methodology to construct and automatically validate a large-scale multimodal dataset for disaster response. Starting from the vision-only Incidents1M, we successfully recovered 100,000 images and generated high-fidelity textual descriptions using two distinct Qwen3.5 architectures: a 4B dense model and a 35B Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model. To ensure the generated captions provide reliable semantic anchoring for DFKD, we introduce an image-blind LLM-as-a-Judge validation pipeline leveraging Qwen3.5-9B. By intentionally obscuring the original image from the judge, this evaluator accurately simulates the modality gap of the student model during data-free distillation. Our evaluation across 173,179 label pairs demonstrates a high semantic agreement (78.65/100) between the two architectures. Furthermore, the automated evaluation reveals a conservative captioning behaviour, characterized by a high Precision (77.6%) and low Recall (46.0%). This minimizes the false positive noise, while simultaneously exposing underlying human annotation inconsistencies in the original ground truth. This work provides a scalable, LLM-validated multimodal dataset and a reproducible framework to advance cross-modal knowledge distillation.

</details>


### [76] [MSCM-net: A hyperspectral image classiffcation method based on multi-scale convolution and Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28277)
*Jianjun Chen,Linlin Wang,Lifang Chang,Limin Huo,Shujiang Song,Yanjia Zhao,Mingwei Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSCM-net，一种结合多尺度CNN和Mamba的高光谱图像分类模型，通过多尺度特征提取和双分支聚合模块提升性能并降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像分类在遥感和工程中应用广泛，现有CNN和Transformer方法存在局部约束和计算复杂度高的问题，因此需要创新方法来解决这些限制。

Method: 模型架构结合多尺度CNN和Mamba，包括多尺度特征提取模块（MCSE）和多个堆叠的Mamba块。MCSE模块由多尺度卷积和SENet组成，用于提取不同感受野的局部信息并自动学习通道重要性。此外，提出双分支特征聚合模块，分别提取中心像素的光谱信息和周围像素的空间信息。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的基准数据集上的实验表明，MSCM-net能够实现先进的分类性能，同时降低计算复杂度。

Conclusion: MSCM-net通过集成多尺度CNN的局部特征提取能力和Mamba的长序列建模优势，有效解决了高光谱图像分类中的局部约束和计算复杂度问题，在性能与效率上均取得显著改进。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging is widely used in remote sensing and engineering. Therefore, research on its classification methods is crucial. While CNN and Transformer-based methods have advanced, they still face locality constraints and high computational complexity. To address these issues, we propose an innovative hyperspectral image classification model, MSCM-net. Specifically, first of all, a model architecture combining multi-scale CNN and Mamba is proposed. It consists of a multi-scale feature extraction module (MCSE) and multiple stacked Mamba blocks, which integrates the local feature extraction capability of multi-scale CNN and the long sequence modeling advantage of Mamba. Secondly, the proposed MCSE module consists of multi-scale convolution and SENet. Convolution kernels of different scales extract local information with different receptive fields, enhancing the fusion of spatial and spectral information. Meanwhile, the SENet enables the model to automatically learn the importance of each channel in the multi-scale features. Furthermore, we also propose a dual-branch feature aggregation module, which further effectively extracts and integrates the spectral information contained in the central pixel and the spatial information in the surrounding pixels. Our model has undergone numerous experiments on three widely used benchmark datasets. The experimental results show that MSCM-net can achieve advanced classification performance while reducing computational complexity.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Visual Ambiguity: Guiding Robust Monocular Depth Estimation in Challenging Scenarios via Detailed Long Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28285)
*Junrui Zhang,Jiaqi Li,Yiran Wang,Liao Shen,Zhiguo Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation (MDE) faces challenges with non-Lambertian surfaces and adverse weather conditions due to the visual ambiguities inherent in single-image limited information. Existing works address them in isolation via image inpainting or augmentation, yielding limited robustness gains. Language, as a powerful complementary modality to vision, is demonstrated to enhance the visual perception capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) via detailed long captions. However, prior language-integrated MDE methods fail to fully harness this potential due to short text input with limited information, coarse global text feature learning, and limited language guidance during depth decoding. To address these limitations, we propose CapDepth, a novel framework for robust MDE that leverages guidance from detailed long captions to alleviate visual ambiguities in both challenging scenarios. First, we design a detailed long caption input template that explicitly conveys rich spatial relationships among multiple atom sentences. Second, a dynamic caption encoder is introduced to extract fine-grained depth-relevant text features via progressive masked attention. Finally, we propose a text-adaptive decoder that guides enhanced depth decoding with text features via stable adaptive layer normalization. Extensive experiments validate the efficacy of CapDepth, which outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving depth error reductions of 25.0% on non-Lambertian surfaces and 22.0% under adverse weather conditions.

</details>


### [78] [MonoVoc: Decoupling Geometry and Semantics for Lightweight Monocular Open-Vocabulary 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28300)
*Pouya Ardekhani,Zahra Dehghanian,Morteza Abolghasemi,Hamid R. Rabiee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无训练、解耦几何与语义的开放词汇3D场景理解方法，输入单目视频，输出紧凑的物体级语义高斯地图，显著降低内存使用并保持竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇3D场景理解对下一代交互系统至关重要，但当前基于3D高斯的框架存在多视图捕捉要求严格、场景优化成本高昂、以及存储密集语言特征带来巨大内存开销的瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种无训练的流程，将3D几何重建与语义集成显式解耦。以标准单目视频序列作为输入，独立进行几何提取，并通过轻量级模块化的后处理框架进行语义 grounding，生成物体级的语义高斯地图。

Result: 在Replica数据集上的广泛评估表明，该解耦架构保持了强大的渲染保真度和具有竞争力的分割精度。关键的是，通过用模块化的物体级语义嵌入取代密集的逐高斯存储，该方法相比当前最优基线实现了内存使用量级的降低。

Conclusion: 该方法为直接从日常单目视频进行高效的开放词汇3D检索和问答提供了一个高度实用、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Open vocabulary 3D scene understanding is essential for next-generation interactive systems, empowering users to intuitively query and navigate reconstructed environments using natural language. However, current 3D Gaussian frameworks are often bottlenecked by restrictive multiview capture requirements, costly scene-specific optimization, and the massive memory overhead of storing dense language features. We present a novel, training-free pipeline that fundamentally reimagines this paradigm by explicitly decoupling 3D geometric reconstruction from semantic integration. Given a standard monocular video sequence as input, our method efficiently outputs a compact, highly interpretable, and fully searchable object-level semantic Gaussian map. Rather than entangling heavy language embeddings within the mapping loop, we extract geometry independently and ground semantics through a lightweight, modular post-processing framework. Extensive evaluations on the Replica dataset demonstrate that this decoupled architecture preserves strong rendering fidelity and competitive segmentation accuracy. Crucially, by replacing dense per-Gaussian storage with modular, object-level semantic embeddings, our approach delivers an order-of-magnitude reduction in memory usage compared to SOTA baselines. This provides a highly efficient, scalable, and practical solution for open-vocabulary 3D retrieval and question answering directly from everyday monocular video.

</details>


### [79] [ObjectStream: Latent Objects as Memory Anchors for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28312)
*Mingkang Dong,Muxin Pu,Jie Li,Bohan Guo,Songruo Chen,Bin Ren,Xu Zheng,Chen Zhao,Tianwen Qian,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yuqian Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ObjectStream是一个无训练框架，将潜在对象作为流式视频理解的记忆锚点，通过从冻结Video-LLM表示中诱导空间连贯的对象并维护其历史，在无需外部检测器的情况下显著提升在线流式和离线长视频理解的效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法主要根据token重要性、时间冗余或段级相关性来管理增长的视觉上下文，很少围绕在时间上持续存在和演化的对象来组织证据，这限制了模型对对象身份、交互和状态变化的推理能力。

Method: 该框架直接从冻结的Video-LLM表示中诱导出空间连贯的潜在对象，无需外部检测器或分割模型；将这些对象跨帧链接为持久锚点，并在有限的内存预算下维护其历史；系统保留三种互补证据形式：持久对象历史、瞬时对象变化和近期视觉上下文，从而在不改变底层Video-LLM的情况下支持对象级推理。

Result: 在在线流式评估中，ObjectStream将Qwen2.5-VL-7B在OVO-Bench实时视觉感知基准上的得分提升了10.0分，同时将峰值GPU内存和首个token生成时间（TTFT）降低了约50%；在离线长视频基准测试中，即使丢弃了82.5%的视觉token，其性能也超越了使用全token的基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，潜在对象可以作为组织紧凑流式视频记忆的一种实用且有效的原则；ObjectStream框架在不增加训练成本和外部模型依赖的前提下，显著提升了Video-LLMs在流式和长视频场景中的理解能力与效率。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to continuously retain useful visual evidence before future questions are known. Existing approaches primarily manage the growing visual context according to token importance, temporal redundancy, or segment-level relevance, but rarely organize evidence around objects that persist and evolve over time. Thus, in this paper, we introduce ObjectStream, a training-free framework that treats latent objects as memory anchors for streaming video understanding. ObjectStream induces spatially coherent latent objects directly from frozen Video-LLM representations, links them across frames into persistent anchors, and maintains their histories under a bounded memory budget, without requiring external object detectors or segmentation models. Built on these anchors, ObjectStream preserves three complementary forms of evidence: persistent object histories, transient object changes, and recent visual context. This design enables existing Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) to reason over object identities, interactions, and state changes while leaving the underlying model unchanged. Extensive experiments on online streaming and offline long-video benchmarks demonstrate both effectiveness and efficiency. In online streaming evaluation, ObjectStream improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 10.0 points on OVO-Bench Real-Time Visual Perception, while reducing peak GPU mem-ory and TTFT by approximately 50%. On offline long-video benchmarks, it surpasses the full-token baseline while discarding 82.5% of visual tokens. These results highlight latent objects as a practical and effective organizing principle for compact streaming video memory.

</details>


### [80] [AdaAnchor4D: Anchor-Conditioned Spatiotemporal Feature Aggregation for Monocular UAV 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28320)
*Peiyi Xu,Junpeng Zhang,Guanbin Li,Ronghua Shang,Mingtao Feng,Le Dong,Weisheng Dong,Guangming Shi,Jie Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机单目视频动态场景重建中存在的时空异质性问题，提出了一种自适应锚点变形框架AdaAnchor4D，通过自适应特征聚合和解耦变形技术，在多个数据集上实现了比现有动态高斯方法更高的渲染质量并保持了实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分解共享时空特征场的方法，其固定的平面特征组合机制难以适应无人机城市场景中显著的时空异质性（不同区域有不同活动模式，动态区域运动状态会随时间演变），这常导致重影伪影和动态细节模糊。因此，需要一种能适应局部动态变化的重建方法。

Method: 提出了AdaAnchor4D框架，核心包含三个技术：1) 锚点条件特征聚合（ACFA）：利用锚点特定的聚合嵌入和时序信息自适应聚合共享时空特征，为不同局部单元生成适应其局部和时序状态的动态表示。2) 解耦局部几何变形（DLGD）：将锚点状态变形与局部高斯几何变形分离。3) 密度自适应坐标翘曲（DACW）：根据锚点沿轴向的分布重新参数化特征查询坐标，以缓解非均匀几何采样与均匀网格参数化之间的不匹配。

Result: 在UAV-Arc4D、VisDrone和UAVDT数据集上的实验表明，AdaAnchor4D在保持实时渲染性能的同时，比代表性动态高斯方法实现了更高的渲染质量。

Conclusion: AdaAnchor4D通过其自适应的特征聚合和几何变形机制，有效解决了无人机场景重建中的时空异质性挑战，提升了动态细节的重建质量，同时维持了实时渲染效率，代码将开源。

Abstract: Monocular UAV videos provide valuable observations for dynamic reconstruction of complex urban scenes. However, such scenes exhibit pronounced spatiotemporal heterogeneity: different regions follow distinct temporal activity patterns, while the motion states of some dynamic regions may further evolve over time. Although dynamic Gaussian methods based on decomposed shared spatiotemporal feature fields have achieved efficient and accurate reconstruction in object-centric or relatively compact scenes, their commonly adopted fixed plane-wise feature combination mechanisms are less suited to the heterogeneous local dynamics of UAV scenes, often leading to ghosting artifacts and blurred dynamic details. To address this challenge, we propose AdaAnchor4D, an adaptive anchor deformation framework for monocular UAV dynamic scene reconstruction. At its core, Anchor-Conditioned Feature Aggregation (ACFA) adaptively aggregates shared spatiotemporal features using anchor-specific aggregation embeddings and temporal information, allowing different local units to obtain dynamic representations tailored to their local and temporal states. Decoupled Local Geometry Deformation (DLGD) separates anchor-state deformation from local Gaussian geometry deformation, while Density-Adaptive Coordinate Warping (DACW) reparameterizes feature-query coordinates according to the axis-wise anchor distributions, alleviating the mismatch between non-uniform geometric sampling and uniform grid parameterization. Experiments on UAV-Arc4D, VisDrone, and UAVDT show that AdaAnchor4D achieves higher rendering quality than representative dynamic Gaussian methods while maintaining real-time rendering performance. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [81] [Same Branches, Different Trees: A Bifurcation Connectedness Metric for Coronary Artery Segmentation and FFR-CT Decision Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28327)
*Maame Owusu-Ansah,Kelvin Lee,Dr Vinod Venugopal,Muhammad Moazzam Jawaid,Wenting Duan,James Brown*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Fractional flow reserve derived from CT angiography (FFR-CT) simulates flow through a patient-specific vessel model, so its accuracy depends on the connectedness of the segmented tree, not only on volumetric overlap: a segmentation can reach high Dice yet sever a bifurcation, dropping the downstream subtree and reversing the treatment decision. Topology-aware losses such as clDice and Skeleton Recall act on the global centreline and can miss localised breaks. We study the Bifurcation Connectedness Score (BCS), which scores connectedness at each ground-truth bifurcation, and soft-BCS, its differentiable training surrogate. BCS captures a property of segmentation quality the standard metrics miss: it responds strongly to breaks in connectedness while staying largely unchanged under connectedness-preserving narrowing. Higher BCS accompanies closer agreement between the FFR-CT decisions a solver makes on predicted versus ground-truth geometry, most clearly in severe disease (OR 2.16, CI [1.23, 4.18]). Both decisions come from the same solver, so this reflects geometric, not clinical, fidelity. In training, soft-BCS and Skeleton Recall recover the same branches but build different trees. Recovering branches and keeping them connected are separable properties, so we recommend reporting a measure of each.

</details>


### [82] [Capturing Token Tendencies for Training-Free Token Pruning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28341)
*Jie Ma,Zhike Qiu,Jie Gao,Jiayi Ji,Qian Chen,Xiaoshuai Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为趋势感知剪枝的新框架，通过建模注意力流动的动态轨迹来优化多模态大语言模型的视觉令牌剪枝，在大幅减少令牌数量的同时保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型中的无训练视觉令牌剪枝方法存在关键局限：它们依赖静态、瞬时启发式方法进行不可逆过滤，忽略了模型的层次性和令牌重要性在层间的动态演变。这导致浅层估计过早丢弃了对深层推理至关重要的令牌，造成关键视觉线索的丢失。

Method: 将剪枝从局部快照决策提升为时间轨迹建模问题。通过捕捉注意力流动的动量而非依赖孤立分数，建立动态校正机制，有选择地重新激活那些最初被低估但语义重要性上升的“晚开花”令牌，从而防止关键视觉信息丢失。

Result: 在多种多模态任务上的大量实验表明，该方法实现了更优的效率-性能平衡。具体地，它将视觉令牌减少了超过77.8%，最终层仅保留约23个令牌，同时保持了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的趋势感知剪枝框架为高效多模态推理提供了一种稳健且可逆的解决方案，有效解决了现有静态剪枝方法的局限性，实现了显著的效率提升和性能保持。

Abstract: While visual token pruning is essential for efficient Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), existing training-free methods suffer from a critical limitation: they rely on static, instantaneous heuristics to perform irreversible filtering. This approach ignores the hierarchical nature of MLLMs, where token importance often evolves dynamically rather than remaining fixed across layers. Consequently, tokens essential for deep-layer reasoning are often prematurely discarded by shallow-layer estimates. To address this, we propose Trend-aware Pruning, a novel framework that elevates pruning from a local snapshot decision to a temporal trajectory modeling problem. Instead of relying on isolated scores, our method captures the momentum of attention flow. This enables a dynamic rectification mechanism that selectively reactivates "late-blooming" tokens, those initially undervalued but exhibiting rising semantic importance, thereby preventing the loss of critical visual cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves a superior efficiency-performance trade-off across diverse multimodal tasks. Notably, it reduces visual tokens by over 77.8%, retaining only approximately 23 tokens in the final layer while maintaining competitive performance, offering a robust and reversible solution for high-efficiency multimodal inference.

</details>


### [83] [ShadowDancer: Teaching Video World Models Any Action by Learning Unified Dynamics Representations from a Video and Its Shadow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28362)
*Jin Cao,Zian Meng,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShadowDancer 提出了一种创新的“影子对”和“跨影子预测”方法，实现了在多样动态下对视频世界模型的任意动作、帧级精确控制，无需动作标签或微调即可将演示视频作为可重用动作资产迁移到新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型在动作控制上存在表示性障碍：要么动作编码模糊，依赖模型自行推演；要么通过难以获取的结构化信号进行精确编码，导致跨多样动态的精确控制不切实际。演示视频虽能指定动态，但仅通过单一外观展现，所学动态迁移到新场景效果差。

Method: 1. 提出“影子对”：通过构建的“影子库”大规模生成独立重采样外观但回放相同动态的视频对，使动态族在其可构造时变得可控。2. 提出“跨影子预测”：通过从一个影子预测另一个来学习动作，使配对中重采样的部分被丢弃，保留的部分成为动作，形成统一的动态表示，并驱动一个块因果世界模型。

Result: 实验表明，ShadowDancer 在多样动态族中，相比强大的潜在动作和交互式世界模型基线，在动作转移和长时序动作推出方面均有提升，在推出比较中平均盲测胜率达86%。

Conclusion: ShadowDancer 通过影子对和跨影子预测，有效解决了从演示视频学习可迁移动作表示的难题，实现了无需动作标签、运动估计器或微调的精确帧级控制，显著提升了交互式视频世界模型的动作控制与泛化能力。

Abstract: We present ShadowDancer, a novel approach to any-action, frame-level control of interactive video world models. The obstacle is representational: existing interfaces either encode an action loosely, leaving how it unfolds for the model to improvise, or encode it exactly through structured signals that serve one family and are hard to acquire, so precise control across diverse dynamics remains impractical. Demonstration videos are the natural remedy, specifying any dynamics frame by frame; yet a video shows its dynamics only through one particular appearance, a single shadow of the underlying dynamics, so actions learned from demonstrations transfer poorly to new scenes. ShadowDancer addresses this with two key innovations: (1) shadow pairs, video pairs that replay the same dynamics under independently resampled appearance, constructed at scale by our Shadow Library, so that a dynamics family becomes controllable exactly when such pairs can be constructed for it; and (2) cross-shadow prediction, which learns actions by predicting one shadow from the other, so that whatever the pairing resamples is discarded by construction and whatever it preserves becomes the action, yielding a unified dynamics representation that drives a block-causal world model. Any demonstrated clip thus becomes a reusable action asset, replayed in new environments without action labels, motion estimators, or fine-tuning. Experiments demonstrate improved action transfer and long action rollout over strong latent-action and interactive world model baselines across diverse dynamics families, with an average blinded win rate of 86% in rollout comparisons. We show video results at https://ShadowDancer-1.github.io

</details>


### [84] [Explaining Image Similarity with Automatically Extracted Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28386)
*Isaac Roberts,Petra Bevandic,Alexander Schulz,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型和度量无关的框架，利用通过稀疏自编码器自动提取的概念激活向量来解释图像相似性。该方法通过沿概念方向扰动图像嵌入来量化概念重要性，并支持图像对和群组级的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像相似性可解释性方法（如基于梯度的归因图）只能提供局部解释，难以揭示驱动相似性的全局性因素（如纹理、形状或颜色）。需要一种能理解模型在嵌入空间中如何做出相似性判断的方法。

Method: 1. 使用稀疏自编码器从嵌入中自动发现概念激活向量。2. 对图像对的嵌入进行概念方向扰动，通过相似性函数的变化计算概念重要性，并生成概念归因图。3. 将该方法扩展到群组级相似性解释。4. 引入“示例检索”方法，用于找回具有相似原因的样本。

Result: 实验表明：1. 潜在空间扰动比像素空间基线方法更忠实于底层数据分布。2. 计算出的概念重要性能够线性地恢复真实的相似性分数。定性结果证实了该方法在理解模型对个体和群组相似性判断方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的框架是一个模型和度量无关的通用解决方案，能够通过概念激活向量为图像相似性提供可解释的、全局性的见解，不仅适用于图像对，也适用于图像群组，并有助于理解模型的相似性判断。

Abstract: Image similarity underlies many computer vision applications, yet it is often unclear why two images receive a high or low similarity score. Existing explainability methods often rely on gradient-based attribution maps to provide local justifications for similarity. These approaches struggle to provide global insights into what specifically drives similarity in regions of an embedding space, such as texture, shape, or color. We introduce a model- and metric-agnostic framework that explains image similarity using Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) extracted automatically via Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). Given a pair of images, we perturb their embeddings along discovered concept directions and measure the resulting change in a chosen similarity function, yielding concept importances. For image pairs, we provide localization with concept attribution maps. We extend this procedure to group-level settings, explaining what drives similarity across a cluster of images rather than a single pair, and further, we introduce Exemplar Retrieval, aiming to recover samples with similar reasons contributing to similarity. Our experiments show that our latent perturbations are more faithful to the underlying data distribution than pixel-space baselines, and that concept importances linearly recover the true similarity score. Qualitative results further confirm the usefulness of our methods in understanding a model's individual and group similarity judgments.

</details>


### [85] [Hand-Object Interaction in the Age of Large Foundation Models:Reconstruction, Generation, and Embodied Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28394)
*Weiquan Lin,Yu Deng,Shiyang Liu,Luping Xiao,Xu Tang,Junzhi Yu,Jiaolong Yang,Lei Zhang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了基础模型的先验知识如何赋能手物交互建模，并提出了一个包含八类先验的分类体系，涵盖几何、语义和视觉三大方面，同时分析了HOI在机器人学习中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手物交互建模面临多重挑战，需要对手部关节、物体几何、接触、语义和动力学进行联合推理，且存在严重的视觉不确定性。基础模型通过从大规模跨域数据中学习可迁移的先验知识，为解决这些挑战提供了新途径。然而，现有文献零散，缺乏对所引入知识、其在HOI管道中的引入位置及其解决的不确定性的系统化描述。

Method: 本文对基础模型在手物交互中的先验进行了首次系统综述。文献被组织为涵盖重建与生成的六个HOI任务。更关键的是，建立了一个由八个基础模型子先验组成的分类体系，分为几何、语义和视觉三大类。几何先验包括形状检索、形状重建和空间重建；语义先验包括语义接地和语言推理；视觉先验涵盖视觉表征、图像生成和视频生成。基于此分类体系，系统分析了不同先验在HOI管道和任务中的表示、注入和适配方式。

Result: 研究结果表明，基础模型为HOI任务提供了关键的可迁移先验知识。通过提出的分类体系，本文系统地梳理和阐明了这些先验如何在具体的HOI任务（如手部重建、物体位姿估计、抓取合成、动作理解、交互生成等）中被应用以降低不确定性。此外，本文还考察了HOI衍生知识如何被用于机器人学习，包括基于人类数据的预训练、从人类到机器人的技能迁移以及从HOI到机器人数据的生成。

Conclusion: 本文系统总结了基础模型如何通过引入几何、语义和视觉先验来增强手物交互建模，并探讨了其在机器人学习中的反向应用。文章最后总结了相关数据集与评估协议，讨论了当前局限性，并指出了通向更具泛化能力的HOI系统的未来研究方向。为支持长期进展，策划并维护了一个持续收录新兴方法和基准的实时资源库。

Abstract: Hand-object interaction (HOI) modeling remains challenging because it requires joint reasoning about hand articulation, object geometry, contact, semantics, and dynamics under severe visual uncertainty. Foundation models introduce transferable prior knowledge learned from large-scale cross-domain data, offering new ways to address these challenges beyond task-specific data and models. However, the rapidly growing literature remains fragmented, and existing studies typically describe these methods simply as ``using large models'' without systematically characterizing what knowledge is introduced, where it enters the HOI pipeline, or which HOI uncertainty it helps reduce. This survey presents the first systematic review of foundation-model priors for HOI. We organize the literature into six HOI tasks spanning reconstruction and generation. More importantly, we establish a taxonomy of eight foundation-model sub-priors grouped into geometric, semantic, and visual families. Geometric priors encompass shape retrieval, shape reconstruction, and spatial reconstruction; semantic priors include semantic grounding and language reasoning; and visual priors cover visual representation, image generation, and video generation. Based on this taxonomy, we systematically analyze how different priors are represented, injected, and adapted across HOI pipelines and tasks. Beyond how foundation models empower HOI, we further examine how HOI-derived knowledge is used in robot learning, including human-data pretraining, human-to-robot skill transfer, and HOI-to-robot data generation. Finally, we summarize datasets and evaluation protocols, and discuss limitations and future directions toward more generalizable HOI systems. To support long-term progress, we curate a live repository that continuously aggregates emerging methods and benchmarks.

</details>

*Ilya Novikov,Svetlana Illarionova,Ruslan Dzharkinov,Maria Smirnova,Ayrat Abdullin,Anna Korotkova,Mariia Ulianova,Dmitrii Shadrin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Abstract: Effective flood monitoring is critical for minimizing the impacts of flood disasters on populations and infrastructure. Yet reliable remote sensing across extensive and environmentally diverse regions remains challenging, as most segmentation algorithms lack the generalisation capacity required for large-scale application, while annotated flood data are scarce and unevenly distributed.
  This study presents an end-to-end multimodal framework for Russian Federation territories sustainable flood monitoring and damage assessment based on synthetic aperture radar data, multispectral imagery, and digital elevation models with their derivatives, forming a 21-channel input. Using a self-collected multimodal dataset covering seven Russian regions, two strategies for water surface detection under limited data conditions were compared: a supervised U-Net++ model and the self-supervised AnySat architecture pre-trained and fine-tuned for the segmentation task. Under the data conditions of this study, supervised learning proved more effective, while the AnySat-based approach offered greater stability and retains advantages for settings where larger unlabelled data or missing modalities at inference are expected.
  The best flood area predictions were used to estimate flood impact in urban areas in terms of the area affected, material damage, casualties, and ecological and agricultural impact. The estimations were conducted following the official methodology of the Russian Ministry of Emergency Situations. Applied to the 2019 Tulun flood, the obtained results closely matched official assessments, except for material damage, due to the open-source databases usage. The results demonstrate the potential of deep learning and multimodal satellite data integration for scalable, reliable flood monitoring across diverse environmental and data-limited conditions.

</details>


### [87] [Negative controls reveal volume-driven confounding in radiomics and imaging foundation model features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28423)
*Katy L. Scott,Sejin Kim,Joshua Siraj,Caryn Geady,Matthew Boccalon,Mattea Welch,Mogtaba Alim,Andrew J. Hope,Benjamin Haibe-Kains*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了READII-2-ROQC框架，用于评估放射组学和深度影像特征是否捕获了独立于肿瘤体积和成像伪影的生物学信号。该框架通过生成体积保持的负对照图像来检验特征的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射组学和影像基础模型有望提供肿瘤生物学的非侵入性生物标志物，但现有预测特征可能反映的是肿瘤体积或采集伪影，而非有意义的图像结构。需要一种方法来评估这些特征是否真正捕捉到了独立的空间信号。

Method: READII-2-ROQC是一个开源框架，利用体积保持的负对照来评估特征。它使用可配置的随机化策略，在肿瘤、背景和整个图像区域生成体素扰动图像，然后比较原始图像与控制图像之间的特征行为和模型性能。

Result: 将该框架应用于三个公开的癌症影像队列，处理了3552个肿瘤体积，从原始图像和九个匹配的对照图像中提取了PyRadiomics和基础模型特征。在重现已发表的生存和HPV状态特征时发现：多个模型在空间结构被破坏后仍保持性能（揭示了体积驱动或上下文混淆），而其他模型则显示出对扰动敏感的信号。

Conclusion: READII-2-ROQC为开发可解释的、基于生物学的影像生物标志物以及可重复的放射组学工作流程，提供了一种可扩展的质量控制策略。

Abstract: Radiomics and imaging foundation models promise non-invasive biomarkers of tumour biology, yet predictive signatures may reflect tumour volume or acquisition artifacts rather than meaningful image structure. We introduce READII-2-ROQC, an open-source framework that uses volume-preserving negative controls to assess whether radiomic and deep imaging features capture independent spatial signals. READII-2-ROQC generates voxel-perturbed images across tumour, background and whole-image regions using configurable randomization strategies, then compares feature behaviour and model performance between original and control images. Applied to three public cancer imaging cohorts, the framework processed 3,552 tumour volumes and extracted PyRadiomics and foundation-model features from original images and nine matched controls. Reproducing published survival and HPV-status signatures, we show that multiple models retain performance after spatial structure is destroyed, revealing volume-driven or contextual confounding, whereas others show perturbation-sensitive signal. READII-2-ROQC provides a scalable quality-control strategy for developing interpretable, biologically grounded imaging biomarkers and reproducible radiomics workflows.

</details>


### [88] [ViewMind3D: Modular View-Aware Inference for Training-Free 3D-QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28442)
*Ping-Kun Chiang,Kun-Ru Wu,Po-han Li,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed
